  actionType: example

    generatedBy: example

    sourceUri: example
  status: Unknown
  tags:

    value: example

SageMakerDomain # CLOUD CONTROL · AWS::SageMaker::Domain

SageMakerDomain manages AWS::SageMaker::Domain through the AWS Cloud Control API.

SageMakerEndpoint # CLOUD CONTROL · AWS::SageMaker::Endpoint

SageMakerEndpoint manages AWS::SageMaker::Endpoint through the AWS Cloud Control API.

kubectl get sagemakerendpoints · example on GitHub

Example

# SageMakerEndpoint — SageMaker
# Full-options example generated from the CRD schema. Optional fields are
# included with sample values; delete what you don't need. Field reference:
# https://konfig-konector.io/docs/sagemaker.html#sagemakerendpoint
apiVersion: aws.konfig.io/v1alpha1
kind: SageMakerEndpoint
metadata:
  name: example-sagemakerendpoint
  namespace: default
spec:
  deploymentConfig:
    autoRollbackConfiguration:
      alarms:
      - alarmName: example-sagemakerendpoint
    blueGreenUpdatePolicy:
      maximumExecutionTimeoutInSeconds: 600
      terminationWaitInSeconds: 1
      trafficRoutingConfiguration:
        type: example
        waitIntervalInSeconds: 1
    rollingUpdatePolicy:
      maximumBatchSize:
        type: example
        value: 1
      maximumExecutionTimeoutInSeconds: 600
      rollbackMaximumBatchSize:
        type: example
        value: 1
      waitIntervalInSeconds: 1
  endpointConfigName: example-sagemakerendpoint
  endpointName: example-sagemakerendpoint
  excludeRetainedVariantProperties:
  - variantPropertyType: example
  providerRef:
    name: example-sagemakerendpoint
    region: us-east-1
  retainAllVariantProperties: false
  retainDeploymentConfig: false
  tags:
  - key: example
    value: example

FieldTypeRequiredDescription

deploymentConfig.blueGreenUpdatePolicy.terminationWaitInSecondsinteger—The wait time before terminating the old endpoint during a blue/green deployment.

deploymentConfig.blueGreenUpdatePolicy.trafficRoutingConfiguration.canarySize.typestringyesSpecifies whether the `Value` is an instance count or a capacity unit.
deploymentConfig.blueGreenUpdatePolicy.trafficRoutingConfiguration.canarySize.valueintegeryesThe value representing either the number of instances or the number of capacity units.

FieldTypeRequiredDescription
asyncInferenceConfigobject—Specifies configuration for how an endpoint performs asynchronous inference.
asyncInferenceConfig.clientConfigobject—Configures the behavior of the client used by SageMaker to interact with the model container during asynchronous inference.

asyncInferenceConfig.outputConfig.s3OutputPathstring—The Amazon S3 location to upload inference responses to.

explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.enableExplanationsstring—A JMESPath boolean expression used to filter which records to explain.

explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.inferenceConfig.labelAttributestring—A JMESPath expression used to locate the list of label headers in the model container output.
explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.inferenceConfig.labelHeadersarray<string>—For multiclass classification problems, the label headers are the names of the classes.
explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.inferenceConfig.labelIndexinteger—A zero-based index used to extract a label header or list of label headers from model container output in CSV format.
explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.inferenceConfig.maxPayloadInMBinteger—The maximum payload size (MB) allowed of a request from the explainer to the model container.
explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.inferenceConfig.maxRecordCountinteger—The maximum number of records in a request that the model container can process when querying the model container for the predictions of a synthetic dataset.
explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.inferenceConfig.probabilityAttributestring—A JMESPath expression used to extract the probability (or score) from the model container output if the model container is in JSON Lines format.
explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.inferenceConfig.probabilityIndexinteger—A zero-based index used to extract a probability value (score) or list from model container output in CSV format.
explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.shapConfigobjectyesThe configuration for SHAP analysis.

explainerConfig.clarifyExplainerConfig.shapConfig.seedinteger—The starting value used to initialize the random number generator in the explainer.

productionVariants[].managedInstanceScalingobject—SageMakerEndpointConfigManagedInstanceScaling is a nested property type of AWS::SageMaker::EndpointConfig.
productionVariants[].managedInstanceScaling.maxInstanceCountinteger—The maximum number of instances that the endpoint can provision when it scales up to accommodate an increase in traffic.

        compressionType: None

      volumeSizeInGB: 1

        instanceType: example

        sageMakerImageVersionArn: arn:aws:iam::123456789012:role/example-role

        instanceType: example
